#(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n  end : gen_lzc\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n     logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;
    logic lzc_zeroes;

    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;

    lzc #(
        .WIDTH(n-1),
        .MODE(1'b1)
    ) u_lzc(
        .in_i(lzc_operand),
        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),
        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)
    );

    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;
    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;
    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;

    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;
    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);
    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});

/*
PDPU equipped with a fine-grained 5-stage pipeline (pipe1..pipe5)
OPTIMIZATION: defer the +1 of two's complement to the final sum.
  Instead of mantissa_comp = sign ? (~mant + 1) : mant, we use:
    mantissa_xor = sign ? ~mant : mant  (just XOR, no carry chain)
    and count the number of negative signs to add as correction.
  sum_result = csa(mantissa_xor) + num_neg
  This removes the carry-propagating +1 from the pipe2->pipe3 critical path.
*/

);

    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_norm_a, mants_norm_b;

// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized SystemVerilog.
// Based on starting_point.v with critical-path optimization in pipe2->pipe3.
// Optimization: defer the +1 of two's complement to the final CSA tree,
// using a popcount of negative signs as carry-in correction.

`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \
  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \
    __q <= (__d);                    \
  end


package pdpu_pkg;
    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);
        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;
    endfunction

    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);
        begin
            n = n-1;
            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)
                n = n>>1;
        end
    endfunction

endpackage

package cf_math_pkg;

    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);
        longint remainder;
        remainder = dividend;
        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin
            remainder = remainder - divisor;
        end
    endfunction

    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);
        begin
            n = n-1;
            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)
                n = n>>1;
        end
    endfunction

    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);
        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;
    endfunction

endpackage

module fulladder(
    input logic x,y,z,
    output logic sum,carry
);
    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;
    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);
endmodule

module counter_5to3(
    input logic x1,x2,x3,x4,cin,
    output logic sum,carry,cout
);
    assign sum = x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4 ^ cin;
    assign cout = (x1 ^ x2) & x3 | ~(x1 ^ x2) & x1;
    assign carry = (x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & cin | ~(x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & x4;
endmodule

module comparator #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8
)(
    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,
    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,
    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o
);
    logic [1:0] sign_ab;
    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;
    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;

    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};
    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];
    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];
    
    always_comb begin
        case(sign_ab)
            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;
            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;
            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;
        endcase
    end
endmodule

module booth_encoder(
    input [2:0] code,
    output neg,
    output zero,
    output one,
    output two
);
    assign neg = code[2]&(~code[0]) | code[2]&(~code[1]);
    assign zero = ~(|code) | &code;
    assign two = ~code[2]&code[1]&code[0] | code[2]&(~code[1])&(~code[0]);
    assign one = code[1]&(~code[0]) | (~code[1])&code[0];
endmodule

module gen_product #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 16
)(
    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] multiplicand,
    input logic [2:0] code,
    output logic [WIDTH:0] partial_prod,
    output sign
);
    logic neg,zero,one,two;
    booth_encoder u_booth_encoder(
        .code(code),
        .neg(neg),
        .zero(zero),
        .one(one),
        .two(two)
    );

    logic [WIDTH:0] temp_prod;
    always_comb begin
        if(one) begin
            temp_prod = multiplicand;
        end
        else if(two) begin
            temp_prod = multiplicand << 1;
        end
        else begin
            temp_prod = '0;
        end
    end
    assign partial_prod = neg ? (~temp_prod) : temp_prod;
    assign sign = neg;
endmodule

    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_A = 16,

    logic [COUNT-1:0][WIDTH_A:0] temp_prods;
    logic [COUNT-1:0] signs;

    assign codes[0] = multiplier[2:0];

    gen_product #(
        .WIDTH(WIDTH_A)
    ) ua_gen_product(
        .multiplicand(operand_a),
        .code(codes[0]),
        .partial_prod(temp_prods[0]),
        .sign(signs[0])
    );
    assign partial_prods[0] = {~signs[0], signs[0], signs[0], temp_prods[0]};

    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=1;i<COUNT-1;i++) begin
            assign codes[i] = multiplier[2*i+2:2*i];
            gen_product #(
                .WIDTH(WIDTH_A)
            ) ub_gen_product(
                .multiplicand(operand_a),
                .code(codes[i]),
                .partial_prod(temp_prods[i]),
                .sign(signs[i])
            );
            assign partial_prods[i] = {1'b1,~signs[i],temp_prods[i],1'b0,signs[i-1]} << (2*i-2);
        end
    endgenerate

    assign codes[COUNT-1] = multiplier[2*COUNT:2*COUNT-2];
    gen_product #(

[ASSISTANT]
Now let me think about what else to try. The critical path is pipe3→pipe4: CSA tree (17 inputs of 19 bits) + final addition. The CSA tree has many XOR/XNOR stages. 

Let me try a different approach: instead of the complex recursive CSA tree with 4:2 compressors (which have ripple carry chains internally), use a simpler tree of 3:2 compressors (full adders) which have no internal ripple:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_opt1.v', new_string="module csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    // Use 3:2 compressors (full adders) only -- no ripple carry chain\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if(N==3) begin\n            logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n            logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n            genvar i;\n            for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin : fa\n                assign sum[i] = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i];\n                assign carry[i] = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                                  (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                                  (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n            end\n            assign sum_o = sum;\n            assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry, 1'b0};\n        end\n        else begin\n            localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n            localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];\n\n            csa_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) ua_csa_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n [ASSISTANT]
## Summary

### Final Best Design: `design_opt1.v` with cost 812,970 (down from 968,044 baseline)

### Approaches Tried & Results

**What worked best (in order of impact):**

1. **Deferred two's-complement +1 (XOR-only sign inversion)** — Biggest single win (~10% cost reduction, 968K→879K). Instead of `mantissa_comp = sign ? (~mant + 1) : mant` in the pipe2→pipe3 critical path, used `mantissa_xor = sign ? ~mant : mant` (XOR only) and deferred the +1 corrections by counting negative signs (`$countones`) and adding them at the final sum. This removed a carry chain from the critical path.

2. **Simple `*` multiplier replacing Booth+CSA** (~9% area reduction, 1034→979 area, 879K→861K). Replaced the entire `radix4_booth_multiplier`/`gen_prods`/`booth_encoder`/`gen_product` hierarchy with `assign sum_o = operand_a * operand_b; assign carry_o = '0;`. The mantissa is only 4-bit, so a simple multiplier is smaller and sufficient.

3. **Simplified comparator using direct signed comparison** (~5% cost reduction, 861K→813K). Replaced the case-based comparator with `assign result_o = (operand_a > operand_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;`. Yosys maps this more efficiently.

### What Didn't Work / Caused Regressions

- **Simple `>>`/`<<` barrel shifter** — Area dropped but delay increased significantly (845→891ps), net worse. The explicit mux-tree barrel shifter synthesizes to faster logic.
- **Simplified LZC (priority encoder loop)** — Almost broke correctness initially (variable in for loop), and even when fixed, increased delay substantially (845→875ps).
- **CSA tree rewrite using only 3:2 compressors** — Broke correctness; the carry combination logic was buggy. Reverted.
- **Pipeline rebalancing (splitting CSA from final addition)** — Pushed the mantissa_norm+encoder into one stage, creating a 1114ps critical path. Much worse.
- **Simplified mantissa_norm with `<<`** — Slightly worse (815K vs 813K).

### Lessons Learned

1. **Yosys maps behavioral operators (`*`, `>`, `+`) well for small widths** — for 4-bit multiply and 5-bit comparison, simple operators beat hand-crafted logic trees.
2. **Carry-chain deferral is a powerful technique** — removing `+1` from a per-lane critical path and replacing with a single popcount correction at the end of the CSA tree is highly effective.
3. **Explicit mux structures can be faster than behavioral shifts** — the barrel shifter's mux tree outperformed `>>`/`<<` in delay despite more area.
4. **Pipeline rebalancing requires holistic analysis** — moving logic between stages can shift the critical path elsewhere unexpectedly. Would need to try more balanced splits (e.g., split CSA tree depth rather than CSA+add boundary).
5. **Target_delay sweep had minimal impact** — the default 500ps gave the best ADP; higher values increased delay without reducing area enough.

================================================================================
